\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d^{*}\gamma-d^{*}\delta.

Next, consider slice of the projected sub-gradient. The matricization of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})
has the expression,

\displaystyle{~~~}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\right)\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\neq i,k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\times{\mathbf{V}}_{i}),

where
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}:=\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{i}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}
. Hence we have,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot d_{1}\cdots d_{m}+d_{k}^{-}\leq 3\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}.

As for the slice function value difference, under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
, it has for each
k=1,\dots,m
,
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}-d_{k}^{-}\delta

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma-d_{k}^{-}\delta.

Hence, we finish phase one analyses.

Phase Two Analysis

In phase two analyses, we shall assume the event

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}:=\left\{\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}},\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{2{\mathbf{r}}}}\left|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}\left(f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right)\right|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\right\}

holds. Specifically,
Lemma
13
proves
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2})\geq 1-\exp(-\textsf{DoF}/2)
. By event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
and loss function expectation Lemma
14
, when
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq cb_{0}\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}
, we have,

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})]-C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{3b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality is due to
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq C_{1}\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\cdot b_{0}
. The following lemma analyzes Frobenius norm for projected sub-gradient and completes proving Lemma
1
.

Lemma 3

(Upper bound for sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
be Tucker rank at most
{\mathbf{r}}
tensor. Suppose it satisfies
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}
\cdot b_{0}
. Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\in\partial f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})
be the sub-gradient and
{\mathbb{T}}
be the tangent space of
{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
at point
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
. Then under
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
, we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot\sqrt{m+1}\cdot\delta^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

Note that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
has the upper bound

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}}_{=A_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{A_{2}},

where
\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}:=\sup_{{\mathbf{W}}_{j}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{j},r_{j}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{W}}_{1}{\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{W}}_{m}{\mathbf{W}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
.

First consider
A_{1}
.

Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}
achieves
\|\cdot\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}
with orthonormal matrices
{\mathbf{V}}_{k}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{k},r_{k}}
, namely,

\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

and then take
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\frac{1}{2}\delta\cdot{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}
. Then we have
\textrm{rank}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\leq{\mathbf{r}}
. Hence by definition of sub-gradient and by event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rangle\leq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\leq{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})+C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

(8)

With Lemma
14
we have

{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\leq\frac{1}{2\delta}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\frac{\delta}{8}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{G}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Note that insert
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\frac{1}{2}\delta\cdot{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}
into Equation (
8
) and with
b_{0}\geq\delta
,
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq\delta\cdot\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\delta\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}\delta\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}+C\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}},

By solving the sbove quadratic inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}\leq c_{1}\delta^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Second consider
A_{2}
.
Note that
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}
is the
k
-th matricization of some Tucker rank at most
{\mathbf{r}}
tensor. Then by same analysis as
A_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot\delta^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Finally, we have
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(m+1)c_{1}^{2}\delta^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot\sqrt{m+1}\cdot\delta^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

∎

A.2
Proof of Theorem
1

A.2.1
Leave-one-out Sequence

Entrywise normed error in phase one could be obtained directly. However, in phase two, in order to have delicate bound of entrywise normed error, we turn to the powerful leave-one-out framework
[
Chen et al. 2021a
]
. Introduce two sets of the auxiliary loss function
\check{f}_{j}^{(k)}
and
\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}
, for each
k=1,\dots,m
and
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
,

\displaystyle\check{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}):=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{-j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}},

and

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}):=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{-j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}.

(9)

Both
\check{f}_{j}^{(k)}
and
\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}
are free of noise randomness for the
j
-th slice by order
k
and we define the leave-one-out sequence
\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\}
accordingly, see Algorithm
4
. Here,
\check{f}_{j}^{(k)}
is used in phase one while in phase two the leave-one-out sequence is based on
\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}
, see Algorithm
4
.

Algorithm 4

Leave-one-out Sequence

Input
: Same
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}
,
l_{\max}
,
\eta_{l}
as Algorithm
1

Initialization:
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}^{(k),j}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{\mathbf{r}}

for

l=0,\ldots,l_{\max}

do

Choose a vanilla subgradient:
{\mathbf{G}}_{l}^{(k),j}\in\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\partial\check{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j})&&\text{ if in phase one}\\
\partial\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j})&&\text{ if in phase two}\end{array}\right.

Compute Riemannian sub-gradient:
\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{l}^{(k),j}={\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\mathbf{G}}_{l}^{(k),j})

Retraction to
{\mathbb{M}}_{\mathbf{r}}
:
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}=\text{HOSVD}_{\mathbf{r}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\widetilde{\mathbf{G}}_{l}^{(k),j})

end

for

Even though, in phase one, we don’t need the leave-one-out sequence to obtain sharp entrwise norm, in order to have a sequence not related with slice noise in the second phase, we need such a sequence in the first phase. Besides, notice that here for Pseudo-Huber loss, we have two different methods in removing the slice randomness, ignoring the noise or taking expectation and these two methods are equivalent in
\ell_{2}
loss
Chen et al. 2021b
,
Chen et al. 2021a
. Due to phase one and phase two have different analysis framework, the proper type of leave-one-out sequence is taken accordingly.

A.2.2
Phase One

For convenience, denote
D_{l}:=\left(1-\frac{1}{32}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)^{l}\cdot D_{0}
. We shall prove the following Euqation (
10a
)-(
10e
) and (
11a
)-(
11e
) by induction. It’s obvious that it holds for the initialization
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}
. Suppose it holds for iteration
l
and we consider the
(l+1)
-th iteration. As for the original sequence, we are going to prove

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l+1},

(10a)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1},

(10b)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1},

(10c)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l+1},

(10d)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

(10e)

where
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{l+1}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(l+1)},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(l+1)}\rrbracket
is the Tucker decomposition and
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
. As for the leave-one-out sequence, we are going to prove

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l+1}

(11a)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1}

(11b)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1}

(11c)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l+1},

(11d)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1),(k),j}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

(11e)

where
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(l+1),(k),j},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(l+1),(k),j}\rrbracket
is the Tucker decomposition and
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1),(k),j}:=\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1),(k),j}\right)^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
. Notice that phase one regularity conditions Lemma
1
also holds for the leave-one-out sequences
\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\}
under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
in parallel and its convergence analyses are same as the original sequence. Hence we shall only show detailed proof of original sequence and skip the leave-one-out analysis in the first phase.

Frobenius norm

First consider
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

We have analyzed the last term
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
in Lemma
1
that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq d^{*}
.
Note that by definition of sub-gradient and analyses of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
in Lemma
1
, the intermediate term has the following lower bound

\displaystyle\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d^{*}\gamma-d^{*}\delta-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle.

Besides, Lemma
21
shows that
\left|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\right|\leq 8m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
and absolute values of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}
entries are bounded by
1
, which implies
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{d^{*}}
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma+2\eta_{l}d^{*}\delta

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d^{*}}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}.

Then insert
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq D_{l}
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq(5m+1)\cdot\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l}
into the above equation,

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma+2\eta_{l}d^{*}\delta+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d^{*}}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\right)D_{l}^{2}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma+2\eta_{l}d^{*}\delta+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d^{*}}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l}^{2}-2\eta_{l}(5m+1)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}}{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}}D_{l}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma+2\eta_{l}d^{*}\delta+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{d^{*}}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*},

where the second inequality also uses
1-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}>0
. Then with phase one region constraint and initialization condition
D_{l}\leq D_{0}\leq c_{m}\underline{\lambda}^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l}^{2}-\eta_{l}(5m+1)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}}{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}}D_{l}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}.

Note that the stepsize
\eta_{l}\in\frac{1}{8(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l}\cdot\left[1,3\right]
and we could have

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}^{2}.

Recall that
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}=\textrm{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))
and by Theorem
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}=D_{l+1},

where initialization condition
D_{l}\leq D_{0}\leq c\underline{\lambda}^{*}\cdot(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*}r^{*})^{-1}
is used.

Entrywise norm

Consider
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
, for
k=1,\dots,m
or equivalently, consider

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},\quad\text{ for each }j=1,\dots,d_{k}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Insert induction
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
into Lemma
1
and it provides an upper bound for the last term

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq 9\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}.

Then consider the intermidiate term
\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle
. Note that with simple calculations, we obtain

\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle,

and

\begin{split}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle,\end{split}

(12)

where
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}={\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}
is used. With Lemma
21
, we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d_{k}^{-}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left(m^{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\right)=:B_{1},

and

\displaystyle{~~~~}\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left(m^{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\right)=:B_{2}.

Note that by induction
\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l}
, we have
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}
. Lemma
22
and Lemma
16
infer that

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 8\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l}.

In this way, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}\vee B_{2}\leq 16m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{d^{*}}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}D_{l}^{2}.

Also, by definition of sub-gradient and by regularity properties in Lemma
1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{\rm{H}_{p}}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma-d_{k}^{-}\delta.

Thus, the intermediate term has the lower bound

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma-(B_{1}+B_{2}).

Hence combine the above euqations and then we have upper bound for the slice

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+12\eta_{l}d_{k}^{-}\gamma+2\eta_{l}(B_{1}+B_{2})+9\eta_{l}^{2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq 9\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}D_{l}^{2}-18\eta_{l}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}(5m+1)^{-1}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*})^{-1/2}D_{l}+12\eta_{l}d_{k}^{-}\gamma+2\eta_{l}(B_{1}+B_{2})+9\eta_{l}^{2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq 9\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot\left(1-\frac{3}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}^{2},

where the second inequality uses induction of
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
and last line uses phase one region constraint and step size selection, similar to Frobenius norm analyses. The above equation infers that

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}.

Take maximum over
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
, it is exactly

\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}.

Besides, with Lemma
21
and Lemma
23
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 5m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}.

Then it arrives at

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}+5m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2},

where
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})=0
is used. Then by Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+32m\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+32m\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(\mu^{m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}+32m\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l+1}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+5m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1},

and Lemma
19
also infers

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\cdot\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+16D_{l+1}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\right)\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+1.1\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}D_{l+1}

\displaystyle\leq 5D_{l+1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

where the second ineuqality uses

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Note that it implies
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}
is incoherent with
3\mu^{*}
, namely due to,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}D_{l+1}+\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}.

Finally, by Lemma
9
, we have bound of entrywise normed bound

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m-1}r_{k}^{-}}{d_{k}^{-}}}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}D_{l+1}.

Phase One Output

Notice that if the signal-to-noise ratio is smaller than
O(\sqrt{d^{*}})
and then the initialization already guarantees error of scale
O(\sqrt{d^{*}}\gamma)
, in which case it enters phase two directly and doesn’t need the first phase.
Anyway, phase two starts with the error rate of

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\vee\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\min\left\{2(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),D_{0}\right\},

By traingular inequality, we have upper bound of distance between the origanl sequence and leave-one-out sequence,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\min\left\{2(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),D_{0}\right\}.

Also, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\vee\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\min\left\{2(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),D_{0}\right\},

which infers

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 6\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\min\left\{2(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),D_{0}\right\}.

The entry-wise normed distance has the following bound,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 2(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}(6\gamma+\delta),\quad\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 2(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}(6\gamma+\delta).

A.2.3
Phase Two

Analysis of phase two is more delicate. We shall continue from the output of phase one
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}
and prove via induction. Denote
D_{l}:=\left(1-\frac{3}{c_{1}^{2}64(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)^{l-l_{1}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
. Suppose Equation (
13a
)-(
13e
) hold for iteration
l
and we shall prove Equation (
13a
)-(
13e
) with iteration
l+1
for all
k,v=1,\dots,m
and
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
,
i=1,\dots,d_{v}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\vee\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l+1}

(13a)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l+1}

(13b)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\left\{36D_{0},C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\right\}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta

(13c)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(j),k}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\left\{36D_{0},C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\right\}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta

(13d)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\vee\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1),(v),i}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}

(13e)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\vee\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 72(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}(6\gamma+\delta)

(13f)

Frobenius norm

First consider
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

According to Lemma
1
, the last term has the upper bound
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq c_{1}^{2}(m+1)\delta^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
By definition of sub-gradient and analysis of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
in Lemma
1
, the intermediate term has the lower bound

\displaystyle\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

Besides, by Lemma
21
and proofs of Lemma
1
, we have
\left|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\right|\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\|_{\mathrm{F,2{\mathbf{r}}}}\leq 8m^{2}c_{1}\delta^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{3}
and hence we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\eta_{l}\frac{1}{b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+16\eta_{l}m^{2}c_{1}\delta^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{3}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+\eta_{l}^{2}c_{1}^{2}(m+1)\delta^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\eta_{l}\frac{1}{2b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}++\eta_{l}^{2}c_{1}^{2}(m+1)\delta^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{c_{1}^{2}64(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the second inequality is because of
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}^{-1}m^{-2}\frac{\delta}{b_{0}}\underline{\lambda}^{*}
and the last inequality uses stepsize selection
\eta_{l}\in\left[\frac{1}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}},\frac{3}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}}\right]
.
By tensor perturbation Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{c_{1}^{2}32(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq D_{l+1}.

We could have parallel results under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
for leave-one-out sequence
k=1,\dots,m
,
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
,
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq D_{l+1}
and hence we skip its proof.

Entrywise norm

We shall prove Equation (
13b
)-(
13f
) step by step.

Step One
First, consider the
j
-th slice of order
k
in the leave-one-out sequence,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

According to leave-one-out sequence construction and with expectation calculations in proof of Lemma
14
(that is
|{\mathbb{E}}\dot{\rho}_{H_{p}}(t-\xi)|\leq t/\delta
), we know
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\delta^{-2}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. Hence, by the induction of
\left\lVert({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l),(k),j})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
and the regularity properties, the slice of projected sub-gradient term has the following upper bound

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\delta^{-2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+6\delta^{-2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

As for the intermediate term, it has

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle,

where the latter term could be expanded in the following way (see details in phase one analyses Section
A.2.2
),

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle-\underbrace{\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}^{\perp}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle}_{E_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+\underbrace{\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}^{\perp}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle}_{E_{2}}.

Similar to phase one analyses in Section
A.2.2
, we have bound for
|E_{1}|
and
|E_{2}|
,

\displaystyle|E_{1}|\vee|E_{2}|\leq 16m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}D_{l}^{2},

where the induction of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}
is used. Also according to leave-one-out sequence definition and loss function expectation Lemma
14
, it has

\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle\geq\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{(k),j}))-\hat{f}_{j}^{(k)}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\geq b_{0}^{-1}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Thus the intermediate term has the lower bound

\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}))\right\rangle\geq b_{0}^{-1}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-32m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot D_{l}^{2}.

Hence, just like pase one entrywise normed analyses in Section
A.2.2
, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}))\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3\delta^{2}}{64(m+1)b_{0}^{2}}\right)\cdot D_{l}.

(14)

Remark that even though results of Lemma
19
are measured
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
, they also hold if it’s constrained to certain slice which is a byproduct in its proof. Then with
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}=\text{HOSVD}_{{\mathbf{r}}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right)
, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1}.

(15)

Step Two
Consider distance between the original sequence and the leave-one-out sequence,

\begin{split}&{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\\
&=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle\\
&{~~~}+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.\end{split}

(16)

Denote the sub-gradient of the original loss function at leave-one-out iterative as
\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\in\partial f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j})
. Notice that entries of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}
are same as
\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}
except the
j
th slice of order
k
.
The last term of Equation (
16
) could be upper bounded with

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right)\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Note that with definition of Riemannian projections and induction over
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+3\delta^{-2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

and by Lemma
23
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right)\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\delta^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\delta^{-1}D_{l}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

(17)

Claim 1
.

With probability exceeding
1-cd^{*-7}
, the following holds for each
k=1,\dots,m
and
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq C(m+1)\sqrt{r^{*}\log d^{*}},

where
c,C>0
are two constants.

According to Claim
1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq C(m+1)\sqrt{r^{*}\log d^{*}}.

(18)

Thus the last term of Equation (
16
) has upper bound,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4m^{4}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\delta^{-2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-2}D_{l}^{4}+4\delta^{-2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-2}D_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right)^{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C(m+1)^{2}r^{*}\log d^{*}.

As for the intermediate term of Equation (
16
), we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle

\displaystyle{~~~}+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left(\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle.

Remark that second term and last term of the above equation could be upper bounded with

\displaystyle{~~~}\left|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

where
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
is analyzed in Equation (
17
) and similarly

\displaystyle{~~~}\left|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left(\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left(\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

where
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left(\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
is bounded in Equation (
18
). Note that with some simple calculations of the remaining term, we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle,

where the second term has the following expressions (see details in Section
A.2.2
),

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle-\underbrace{\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle}_{F_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~}+\underbrace{\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle}_{F_{2}}.

By same analyses in Section
A.2.2
, we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left|F_{1}\right|\vee\left|F_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq m^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 0.25\delta\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+m^{4}\delta^{-1}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\right)^{2},

whose last line uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. On the other hand, by Lemma
10
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right\rangle\geq\delta\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Thus altogether the intermediate term of Equation (
16
) has lower bound,

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\delta\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-8m^{4}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\delta^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-2}D_{l}^{4}-8m^{4}\delta^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-2}D_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Notice that with
8\eta_{l}\leq\delta
, we have

8\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\eta_{l}\delta\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Hence after inserting value of stepsize
\eta_{l}\in\left[\frac{1}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}},\frac{3}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{\delta^{2}}{b_{0}}\right]
, we have the upper bound for Equation (
16
),

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+4m^{2}\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*2}}\cdot\frac{\delta}{b_{0}}\right)\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}^{2}+32m^{4}\frac{\delta}{b_{0}}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot\frac{D_{l}^{4}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*2}}+\frac{\delta^{4}}{b_{0}^{2}}r^{*}\log d^{*}.

Note that Assumption
2
infers that
b_{0}\geq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\geq r^{*}\gamma\geq r^{*}\delta\sqrt{\log d^{*}}
. Furthermore, take square root of the above equation and it has

\begin{split}&\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\\
&{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\left(1+2m^{4}\frac{\delta}{b_{0}}\cdot\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*2}}\right)\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}+8m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+\delta\\
&{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}+8m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+\delta,\end{split}

(19)

where the last line is due to initialization condition. Then, with slice perturbations (same as Section
A.2.2
analyses), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}+32m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+\delta.

By
D_{l}\geq\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}
and
D_{l+1}\leq D_{l}
, the above equation implies

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l+1}+\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{2}+32m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{D_{l}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l}+\frac{D_{l}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta+\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+m^{2}\cdot\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\left(\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l}+\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\prod_{h=l_{1}}^{l}\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{h}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\left(\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}}+\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}}+\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta\right),

where the last inequality is due to

\displaystyle\prod_{h=l_{1}}^{+\infty}\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{h}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\leq\exp\left(\sum_{h=l_{1}}^{+\infty}\log\left(1+m^{2}\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)\right)\leq\exp\left(\sum_{h=l_{1}}^{+\infty}m^{2}\frac{D_{l}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\cdot D_{l_{1}}\cdot 64\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{\delta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3.

Also, notice that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\}
, where
c_{m}:=72(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}}
.
In this way, we completes showing

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta.

(20)

Step Three
Combine step one by-product Equation (
14
) and step two Equation (
19
) and then we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}))\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l+1}+\delta.

Similarly, Equation (
15
) and Equation (
20
) lead to
