\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k,j)}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l+1}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta.

Hence after taking maximum over
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+3.1\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}},

and

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta.

By Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3.1\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\min\{36D_{0},c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)\}+6\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l_{1}}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta.

Then combined with
D_{0}\leq C\underline{\lambda}^{*}
and with same analyses in Section
A.2.2
, we obtain
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
for each
k=1,\dots,m
. Finally, by Lemma
9
, we have upper bound of the error with respect to
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\infty}
norm,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 72(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}(6\gamma+\delta).

Final Step
We still need to show that leave-one-out sequences also stay in the phase two regions which is characterized in Lemma
1
, namely,
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\lesssim\gamma+\delta
. The proof procedure is similar to bounding
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}
. Hence, details are omitted and we only present the steps. Similar to Step Two, we could prove for any
v=1,\dots,k-1
and any
v=1,\dots,d_{v}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(v),i}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(v),i})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\},

and

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\},

by which we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\eta_{l}\cdot\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(v),i}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j})\right)\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(v),i}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(v),i}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(v),i})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\}+6\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}D_{l+1}+\delta,

and

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{i}^{(v)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(v),i}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\}+6\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}D_{l+1}+2\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}b_{0}}\delta.

By taking maximum over
i=1,\dots,d_{v}
, we aobtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{v}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\}+6\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}D_{l+1}+\delta.

Then by Lemma
19
, we have for each
v=1,\dots,m
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{v}^{(l+1),(k,j)}{\mathbf{H}}_{v}^{(l+1),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}\min\left\{c_{m}\sqrt{\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta),36D_{0}\right\},

which infers
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{v}^{(l+1),(k),j}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{v}}{d_{v}}}

and by Lemma
9
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}^{(k),j}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq 72(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}(6\gamma+\delta).

Phase Two Output

At the end of phase two, it reaches the error rate

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}+l_{2}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\cdot b_{0},\quad\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}+l_{2}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}\max_{k=1,\dots,m}(d_{k}^{-})^{-1/2}\cdot\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\cdot b_{0}.

Proof of Claim
1
.

First consider fixed
j
and
k
. Notice that
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}
is a mean zero tensor and only has non-zero entries on the
j
-th slice of order
k
. For simiplicity, we denote

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j})=\left(\begin{matrix}0&0&\ldots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&&\vdots\\
0&0&\cdots&0\\
x_{1}&x_{2}&&x_{d_{k}^{-}}\\
0&0&\cdots&0\\
\vdots&\vdots&&\vdots\\
0&0&\ldots&0\\
\end{matrix}\right)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{k}\times d_{k}^{-}}.

And denote
{\mathbf{x}}=(x_{1},\dots,x_{d_{k}^{-}})^{\top}
. Recall that
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}^{(k),j}
are independent. Then consider
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)
and by Riemannian projection definition, we obtian

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m+1}c_{i}\left\lVert{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{i}\right\rVert_{2},

where
c_{i}\leq 1
and orthogonal matrices
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{k}^{-}\times r_{k}^{-}}
are independent of
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})
. Also notice that
|x_{i}|\leq 2
. Suppose
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}=[{\mathbf{v}}_{i1},\dots,{\mathbf{v}}_{ir_{k}^{-}}]
are columns of
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}
. The Orlicz norm could be bounded with
\left\lVert{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{il}\right\rVert_{\Psi_{2}}\leq 2
, where Hoeffeding Inequality is used. It leads to
\left\lVert\left\lVert{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\right\rVert_{\Psi_{1}}\leq\sum_{l=1}^{r_{k}^{-}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{x}}^{\top}{\mathbf{v}}_{il}\right\rVert_{\Psi_{2}}^{2}\leq 4r_{k}^{-}
. Thus, we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}^{(k),j}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}^{(k),j}-\bar{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}}^{(k),j}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq C(m+1)\sqrt{r_{k}^{-}\log d^{*}}

holds with probability exceeding
1-cd^{*-8}
. Taking the union over
k=1,\dots,m
and
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
and then we obtain Claim
1
, where
r_{k}^{-}<r^{*}
is used.
∎

Appendix B
Proofs under Heavy-Tailed Noise and Sparse Arbitrary Corruptions

To simplify the notation, we use
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}(\cdot)
to represent mask operator of the
j
th slice of a tensor by the
k
th order, namely,

\displaystyle\left[{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}:=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\left[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}&&\text{ if }i_{k}=j\\
0&&\text{ if }i_{k}\neq j\end{array}\right..

Hence, after simple calculations, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=f\left({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right)-f\left({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right)

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\right\rVert_{1}.

B.1
Proof of Lemma
2

Phase One Analysis

We shall prove phase one properties under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
,

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}:=\left\{\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 3d_{k}^{-}\gamma,\quad\text{ for all }k=1,\dots,m,\;j=1,\dots,d_{k}\right\}.

Specifically, Lemma
8
proves
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1})\geq 1-c\sum_{k=1}^{m}d_{k}(d_{k}^{-})^{-1-\min\{1,\varepsilon\}}
. First consider the projected sub-gradient term. Notice that absolute values of the sub-gradient entries
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}
are bounded by
1
, which leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq d^{*}.

It verifies
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{d^{*}}
. Then consider
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\in\Omega}\left(\left|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|-\left|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}+[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|\right)

\displaystyle{~~~~~}+\sum_{(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\notin\Omega}\left(\left|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|-|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1},

where the inequality use triangle inequality. Note that under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
, it has
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\leq 3d^{*}\gamma
. Then by relationship among
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{1}
,
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\infty}
,
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
in Lemma
7
, we get

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-6d^{*}\gamma.

Also, note that
\#\Omega\leq\alpha d^{*}
and it infers that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}\leq\alpha\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}
. In conclusion, we obtain

f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left(\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\alpha d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-6d^{*}\gamma.

When
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
is low-rank and incoherent, we could have delicate bound for slice of the projected sub-gradient. Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
is the Tucker decomposition. In this way, matricization of the projected sub-gradient is,

\displaystyle{~~~}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\right)\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\neq i,k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\times{\mathbf{V}}_{i}),

where
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}:=\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{i}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}
. Then with the inequality
\left\lVert{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{B}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{A}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\mathbf{B}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot d_{1}\cdots d_{m}=3\mu r_{k}\cdot d_{k}^{-}.

On the other hand, by triangle inequality, the slice loss function has a lower bound

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})\right\rVert_{1}=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}}\left(\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d_{k}^{-}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma,

where the last line uses Lemma
7
and event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
.

Phase Two Analysis

Denote
f_{0}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}):=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}
for simplicity. In phase two analyses, we shall assume the event

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}:=\left\{\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}},\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{2{\mathbf{r}}}}\left|f_{0}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f_{0}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}\left(f_{0}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f_{0}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right)\right|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\right\}

holds. Specifically,
Lemma
13
proves
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2})\geq 1-\exp(-\textsf{DoF}/2)
. Then under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
, we have a lower bound of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})]-C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Besides,

\displaystyle{~~~}{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\right]

\displaystyle{~~~}+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}\right].

Note that Lemma
15
proves
{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\right]\geq b_{0}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. By triangle inequality, Holder Inequality and Lemma
11
, we have

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}\leq\sqrt{\alpha d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)(\mu^{*}\vee\mu)^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

and

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)(\mu^{*}\vee\mu)^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Thus, we obtain the following lower bound of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq b_{0}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-4\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)(\mu^{*}\vee\mu)^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}-C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality is due to the phase two region

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq Cb_{0}\cdot\max\{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}},\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)(\mu^{*}\vee\mu)^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\}.

The following lemma shall inherit notations and assumptions in Lemma
2
. It provides upper bound for the projected sub-gradient and finishes the proof.

Lemma 4

(Upper bound for projected sub-gradient)
.

Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu}
statisfy
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq Cb_{0}\cdot\max\{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}},\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)(\mu^{*}\vee\mu)^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\}
. Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\in\partial f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})
be the sub-gradient and
{\mathbb{T}}
be the tangent space of
{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
at point
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
. Then under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
, we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot\sqrt{m+1}\cdot b_{1}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

Note that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
has the upper bound

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}}_{A_{1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{A_{2}},

where
\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}:=\sup_{{\mathbf{W}}_{j}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{j},r_{j}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{W}}_{1}{\mathbf{W}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{W}}_{m}{\mathbf{W}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
.

First consider
A_{1}
.

Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}
achieves
\|\cdot\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}
with orthogonal matrices
{\mathbf{V}}_{k}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{k},r_{k}}
, namely,

\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},\quad\text{ for all }k=1,\cdots,m.

Then take
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\frac{1}{2}b_{1}\cdot{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}
.
By definition of sub-gradient and by triangular inequality, we have

\begin{split}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rangle\leq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})&=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\\
&~~~+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})\right\rVert_{1}\\
&~~~-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\\
&\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}+2\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{1}.\end{split}

(21)

On the other hand, event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
and Lemma
15
imply that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b_{1}}\left(\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\right)+C\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Also note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq\sqrt{\alpha d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=0.5b_{1}\sqrt{\alpha d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=0.5b_{1}\sqrt{\alpha d^{*}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}.

Insert
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\frac{1}{2}b_{1}\cdot{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}^{\top}
into Equation (
21
) and with
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\geq b_{0}\cdot\max\{\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}},\alpha\sqrt{(m+1)\bar{\mu}^{m}r^{*}d^{*}}\}
,
b_{0}\geq b_{1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}b_{1}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}b_{1}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}+\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}+0.5b_{1}\sqrt{\alpha d^{*}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}+Cb_{1}\sqrt{\textsf{DoF}_{m}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}b_{1}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}^{2}+C\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}.

By solving the sbove quadratic inequality of
\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}\leq c_{1}b_{1}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Second consider
A_{2}
.
Note that
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}
is the
k
-th matricization of some Tucker rank at most
{\mathbf{r}}
tensor.
Then by same analysis trick as
A_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot b_{1}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Finally, we have
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq(m+1)c_{1}^{2}b_{1}^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
, which leads to

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{1}\cdot\sqrt{m+1}\cdot b_{1}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

∎

B.2
Proof of Theorem
2

B.2.1
Phase One

For convenience, denote
D_{l}:=\left(1-\frac{1}{32}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)^{l}\cdot D_{0}
. We shall prove the following Euqation (
22a
)-(
22d
) by induction. It’s obvious that it holds for the initialization
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}
. Suppose it holds for iteration
l
and we consider the
(l+1)
-th iteration. We need to prove

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l+1}

(22a)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1}

(22b)

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 5\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l+1}

(22c)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l+1}

(22d)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}.

(22e)

Frobenius norm

First consider
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

We have analyzed the last term in Lemma
2
, which has
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq d^{*}
.
Note that by definition of sub-gradient and analyses of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
in Lemma
2
, the intermediate term has the lower bound

\displaystyle\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left(\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-6d^{*}\gamma-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

Besides, Lemma
21
shows
\left|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\right|\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 8m^{2}\sqrt{d^{*}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+4\eta_{l}\alpha d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\sqrt{d^{*}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*},

Then insert the induction of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
into the above equation
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq D_{l}
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l}
into the above equation, which is slimiar to pseudo-Huber loss case Section
A.2.2
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l}^{2}-2\eta_{l}(5m+1)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}}{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}}D_{l}+4\eta_{l}\alpha(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}D_{l}+12\eta_{l}d^{*}\gamma

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\sqrt{d^{*}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l}^{2}-\frac{2}{3}\eta_{l}(5m+1)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}}{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}}D_{l}+16\eta_{l}m^{2}\sqrt{d^{*}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq D_{l}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\eta_{l}(5m+1)^{-1}\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}}{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}}D_{l}+\eta_{l}^{2}d^{*},

where the second inequality uses phase one region constraints
D_{l}\geq 12(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}\gamma
, corruption rate
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{12(5m+1)^{2}3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}
and the last line is from initialization condition. Then with the stepsize
\eta_{l}\in\frac{1}{8(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}}\cdot D_{l}\cdot\left[1,3\right]
, we obtain

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}^{2}.

Note that
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}=\textrm{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))
and perturbation bound Theorem
19
implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}=D_{l+1},

where we use
D_{l}<D_{0}\leq c\underline{\lambda}^{*}\cdot(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}
.

Entrywise norm

For each
k=1,\dots,m
, consider
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
, or equivalently, consider

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},\quad\text{ for each }j=1,\dots,d_{k}.

Note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2}^{2}.

With
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
, Lemma
2
provides an upper bound for the last term

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq 9\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}.

Then consider the intermidiate term
\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle
. Note that simple calculations lead to

\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle,

and

\begin{split}\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\\
&=\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle+\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle.\end{split}

(23)

With Lemma
21
, we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{d_{k}^{-}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left(m^{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\right)=:B_{1},

and

\displaystyle{~~~~}\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left(m^{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\right)=:B2.

Note that with induction
\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot D_{l}
, we have
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 4\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}
. Also, Lemma
22
shows

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 8\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}D_{l}.

In this way, we have

\displaystyle B_{1}\vee B_{2}\leq 16m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{d^{*}}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}D_{l}^{2}.

Also, by definition of sub-gradient and by analysis in Lemma
2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d_{k}^{-}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma.

Thus, the intermediate term has the lower bound,

\displaystyle\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-6d_{k}^{-}\gamma-(B_{1}+B_{2})

\displaystyle~~~~~-2\alpha d_{k}^{-}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Hence combine the above euqations and then we have upper bound for the slice

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+4\eta_{l}\alpha d_{k}^{-}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}+12\eta_{l}d_{k}^{-}\gamma+2\eta_{l}(B_{1}+B_{2})+9\eta_{l}^{2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}.

Then insert inductions of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}
into the above equation,

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 9\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}D_{l}^{2}-18\eta_{l}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}(5m+1)^{-1}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*})^{-1/2}D_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+8\eta_{l}\alpha\frac{1}{d_{k}}(5m+1)\sqrt{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}d^{*}}D_{l}+12\eta_{l}d_{k}^{-}\gamma+2\eta_{l}(B_{1}+B_{2})+9\eta_{l}^{2}\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}d^{*}

\displaystyle\leq 9\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}\cdot\left(1-\frac{3}{64}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}^{2},

where the last line uses phase one region constraints, corruption rate and initialization guarantees. It shows

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}.

It also infers

\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}.

Also, notice that,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty},

furthermore, with Lemma
21
and Lemma
23
, we could have the following upper bound for the latter term, (same as phase one under pseudo-Huber loss)

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 8m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}.

Then it arrives at

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(5m+1)^{-2}(3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}+5m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}.

Then by Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+32m\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+32m\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{128}(4m+1)^{-2}(\mu^{*m}r^{*})^{-1}\right)D_{l}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}+32m\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l+1}^{2}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+8m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}D_{l}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3D_{l+1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

where
D_{l}^{2}/\underline{\lambda}^{*}\leq D_{l}\cdot D_{0}/\underline{\lambda}^{*}\leq cD_{l}/(m^{4}\mu^{*m}r^{*})\leq 2cD_{l+1}/(m^{4}\mu^{*m}r^{*})
is used, and

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\cdot\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~}\leq\left(1+16D_{l+1}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\right)\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+1.1\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}D_{l+1}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~}\leq 5D_{l+1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

where the second ineuqality is because

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Note that it implies
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}
is incoherent with
3\mu^{*}
, namely due to,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}D_{l+1}+\sqrt{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}},

where the first inequality is from
\left\lVert({\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)})^{-1}\right\rVert\leq\sqrt{2}
, see Lemma 4.6.3
[
Chen et al. 2021a
]
. Finally, by Lemma
9
, we have the upper bound for the entrywise norm of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
,

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m-1}r_{k}^{-}}{d_{k}^{-}}}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{l+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)\sqrt{\frac{3^{m}\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}D_{l+1}.

B.2.2
Phase Two

Frobenius norm

First consider
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\eta_{l}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle+\eta_{l}^{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

We have analyzed the last term in Lemma
2
that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq c_{1}^{2}(m+1)b_{1}^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
By definition of sub-gradient and analysis of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
in Lemma
2
, the intermediate term has the lower bound

\displaystyle\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle.

Lemma
21
and bound of
\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\|_{\rm{F},{\mathbf{r}}}
in proofs of Lemma
2
infer
\bigg|\left\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}\right\rangle\bigg|\leq 8m^{2}c_{1}b_{1}^{-1}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{3}
and then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\eta_{l}\frac{1}{2b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\eta_{l}^{2}c_{1}^{2}(m+1)b_{1}^{-2}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{64c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality is due to
\eta_{l}\in\left[\frac{1}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{b_{0}},\frac{3}{8c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{b_{0}}\right]
. Then note that since
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\tau_{1}
, for each entry
i_{1},\dots,i_{m}
it has

\displaystyle\left|[\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|\leq\left|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|.

Besides
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{2}}{d^{*}}}\left\lVert\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
. Thus altogether we have

\displaystyle\left|[\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|[\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|,

which is also used in
Cai et al. 2022b
. As a consequence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{3}{64c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Then by perturbation bound Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{32c_{1}^{2}(m+1)}\cdot\frac{b_{1}^{2}}{b_{0}^{2}}\right)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Entrywise norm

Note that with the trimming operations, the entrywise normed error is guaranteed

\displaystyle\left|[\text{Trim}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|[\text{Trim}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|+\left|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tau_{1},

and

\displaystyle\left|[\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|[\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l}))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right|\leq 2\tau_{1}.

Thus we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}(\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\text{Trun}_{\tau_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l_{1}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-\eta_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}_{l})))-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\sqrt{d_{k}^{-}}\tau_{1}.

Furthermore, by Lemma
19
, we get (details of calculations are same as Section
A.2.2
)

\displaystyle\left\lVert({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(l+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 5\sqrt{d_{k}^{-}}\tau_{1}.

Also
\text{Trim}_{\tau_{2}}(\cdot)
guarantees the incoherence of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}
, see Lemma B.6 of
Cai et al. 2022b
, namely,

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(l+1)}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 2\kappa\sqrt{\frac{\tau_{2}}{d_{k}}}.

Finally, by Lemma
9
, we obtain the entrywised norm

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l+1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(5m+1)2^{m}\kappa^{m}\tau_{2}^{m/2}\tau_{1}.

B.3
Proof of Lower Bound Theorem
3

The proof follows Theorem 5.1 in
Chen et al. 2018
. Define

\displaystyle\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}):=\sup\left\{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}:\;\max_{i_{1},\cdots,i_{m}}\textsf{TV}(P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}},P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}})\leq\sigma\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha},\;{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}\right\},

where
P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{j}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}}:=N([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{j}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}},\sigma^{2})
,
j=1,2
is the Gaussian distribution and
\textsf{TV}(\cdot,\cdot)
is the total variation. We shall first prove

\displaystyle\inf_{\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}}\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}}\sup_{\{Q_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\}}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\geq\left(\sum_{k=1}^{m}r_{k}d_{k}+r_{1}\cdots r_{m}\right)\sigma^{2}\vee\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})\right)\geq c,

for some constant
c
and then prove
\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})\geq C\alpha^{2}d^{*}/\mu^{*m}r^{*}
for some
C>0
.

Step One
If the corruption rate satisfies
\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})\leq\left(\sum_{k=1}^{m}r_{k}d_{k}+r_{1}\cdots r_{m}\right)\sigma^{2}
, then the lower bound is
\sigma^{2}\cdot(\sum_{k=1}^{m}r_{k}d_{k}+r_{1}\cdots r_{m})
, which is shown in
Zhang and Xia 2018
. We only need to prove when
\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})\geq\left(\sum_{k=1}^{m}r_{k}d_{k}+r_{1}\cdots r_{m}\right)\sigma^{2}
, it has

\displaystyle\inf_{\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}}\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}}\sup_{\{Q_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\}}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left\lVert\widehat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\geq\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})\right)\geq c.

(24)

There exist
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}
such that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}),\quad\max_{i_{1},\cdots,i_{m}}\textsf{TV}(P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}},P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}})\leq\frac{\alpha^{\prime}}{1-\alpha^{\prime}}\sigma,

for some
0<\alpha^{\prime}\leq\alpha
. Note that for each entry
(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\in[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
, there is
0<\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\leq\alpha^{\prime}
such that

\displaystyle\textsf{TV}(P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}},P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}})=\frac{\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}}{1-\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}}\sigma.

Besides, according to
Chen et al. 2018
, there exist distributions
\tilde{Q}_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(1)}
and
\tilde{Q}_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(2)}
such that

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}})P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}}+\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\tilde{Q}_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(1)}=(1-\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}})P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}}+\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\tilde{Q}_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(2)}.

There exist distributions
Q_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(j)}
,
j=1,2
, such that if random variable
\omega\sim Q_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(j)}
then
\omega+[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{j}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{j}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\sim\tilde{Q}_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(j)}
, where
{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{j}
comprises i.i.d.
N(0,\sigma^{2})
entries. Then construct the corruptions with

[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(j)}\sim(1-\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}})\delta_{0}+\alpha_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}Q_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}^{(j)},\quad j=1,2,

where
\delta_{0}
is the zero distribution. Specifically, if a random variable follows
\delta_{0}
, then it is a.s. zero. Under such corruptions,
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}_{1}:={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{1}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}_{1}
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}_{2}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}_{2}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}_{2}
have the same distribution, in which case
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}
are not identifiable based on observations
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}_{j}
,
j=1,2
. Then Le Cam’s two point testing method
Yu 1997
leads to Equation (
24
).

Step Two
We have

\displaystyle\omega(\alpha,{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}})

\displaystyle=\sup\left\{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}:\;\max_{i_{1},\cdots,i_{m}}\textsf{TV}(P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}},P_{[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}})\leq\sigma\frac{\alpha}{1-\alpha},\;{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq\sup\left\{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}:\;\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\leq 4\sigma^{2}\alpha^{2},\;{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{1},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{2}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu^{*}}\right\}

\displaystyle\geq C\sigma^{2}\cdot\alpha^{2}d^{*}/\mu^{*m}r^{*},

where the last equation follows from
Chen et al. 2021b
and the proof completes.

Appendix C
Proofs of Initialization Theorem
4

Recall that
\tilde{\Omega}
is the support of sparse corruption term
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}
. Denote

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}:=\left\{\|{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}})_{j,\cdot}\|_{0}\leq 3\alpha d_{k}^{-},\quad k\in[m],\;j\in[d_{k}]\right\}

as the event of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}
to be an
\alpha
-fiber sparse tensor. By Chernoff bounds, we have
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}})\geq 1-\sum_{k=1}^{m}d_{k}^{-}\exp(-\alpha d_{k})
. We shall use the fact that for all
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}
, its operator is not larger than the one with its entrywise absolute value, namely,
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}\right\rVert
where
[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{\omega}=|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}]_{\omega}|
. First consider entries of
\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
, for any
(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\in[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
, it has

\displaystyle[\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}

\displaystyle=\left(\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}+[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|\leq\tau\right\}}+\left(\tau\cdot\text{sign}\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|>\tau\right\}}

\displaystyle=\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|\leq\tau,(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\notin\tilde{\Omega}\right\}}+\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}+\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|\leq\tau,(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\in\tilde{\Omega}\right\}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\left(\tau\cdot\text{sign}\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|>\tau,(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\notin\tilde{\Omega}\right\}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\left(\tau\cdot\text{sign}\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)\cdot 1_{\left\{|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|>\tau,(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})\in\tilde{\Omega}\right\}}.

After simple calculations, we have

\displaystyle\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\odot 1_{\{\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}+\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}|\leq\tau,\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}

\displaystyle~~~+\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}|>\tau,\omega\notin\tilde{\Omega}\}}+\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}|>\tau,\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}.

Notice that
{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\odot 1_{\{\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}={\mathcal{P}}_{\tilde{\Omega}^{C}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})
is a mean zero term. Then by Theorem 2.1 in
Auddy and Yuan 2022
, with probability exceeding
1-c_{m}\bar{d}^{-\varepsilon/4}
, the first two terms have the bounded operator norm,

\displaystyle~~~~\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\odot 1_{\{\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}\right\rVert\leq C\|\xi\|_{2}\left(\sqrt{\bar{d}\log\bar{d}}+d^{*1/4}(\log\bar{d})^{1/4}\right).

Then consider the fourth term and with probability exceeding
1-c_{m}\bar{d}^{-\varepsilon/4}

\displaystyle~~~\left\lVert\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}|>\tau,\omega\notin\tilde{\Omega}\}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}|>\tau,\omega\notin\tilde{\Omega}\}}-{\mathbb{E}}\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}|>\tau,\omega\notin\tilde{\Omega}\}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle~~~+\left\lVert{\mathbb{E}}\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}|>\tau,\omega\notin\tilde{\Omega}\}}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\|\xi\|_{4}\left(\sqrt{\bar{d}\log\bar{d}}+d^{*1/4}(\log\bar{d})^{1/4}\right)+\sqrt{d^{*}}\left(\|\xi\|_{4}+\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\right)\frac{\|\xi\|_{4}^{2}}{\tau^{2}},

where the last line is due to Theorem 2.1 of
Auddy and Yuan 2022
and also
{\mathbb{E}}|(\xi+t-\tau\cdot\text{sign}(\xi+t))\cdot 1_{\{|\xi+t|\geq\tau\}}|\leq{\mathbb{E}}|(\xi+t)\cdot 1_{\{|\xi+t|\geq\tau\}}|\leq\sqrt{t^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\xi^{2}}\sqrt{{\mathbb{P}}(|\xi|\geq\tau/2)}
. And as for the third and the last term, which is an
\alpha
-fraction fiber sparse term, according to Lemma
5
, we have
\|\left(\tau\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\odot 1_{\{|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}|>\tau,\omega\in\tilde{\Omega}\}}\|_{\mu^{*}}\leq 2\tau\alpha\sqrt{d^{*}}
.
Thus altogether we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2(\|\xi\|_{4}+\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty})\cdot\left(\sqrt{\bar{d}\log\bar{d}}+d^{*1/4}(\log\bar{d})^{1/4}+\sqrt{d^{*}}\frac{\|\xi\|_{4}^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\right)+2\alpha\tau\sqrt{d^{*}}=:\Lambda

\displaystyle\leq 2(\|\xi\|_{4}+\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty})\cdot\left(\sqrt{\bar{d}\log\bar{d}}+4d^{*1/4}(\log\bar{d})^{1/4}\right)+2\alpha\tau\sqrt{d^{*}},

where
\|\cdot\|_{\mu}\leq\|\cdot\|
is used.
Note that the initialization is
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{(0)}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}\rrbracket=\text{HOSVD}(\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}})
. By tensor perturbation bound
[
Cai et al. 2022b
]
or modifications of Theorem 3 in
Cai and Zhang 2018
with analyses similar to the above one, for each
k=1,\dots,m
, we have
