\displaystyle~~~\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert\vee\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{Q}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert\leq\left\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\|\leq C\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},

where
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0)}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}\top{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
. Furthermore, consider
\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
,

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{m}\hat{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*\top}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right)\times_{j>k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{(0)\top}+\left(\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq m\left\lVert\hat{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}\right\rVert\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\sqrt{r^{*}}\|\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\mu}\leq Cm\kappa\sqrt{r^{*}}\Lambda.

Also, by Lemma
20
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{(0)}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq Cm\kappa\sqrt{r^{*}}\Lambda.

C.1
Leave-one-out Sequence

Introduce leave-one-out sequence: for each
k=1,\dots,m
and
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
, denote
\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{(k),j}:={\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{-j}^{(k)}}(\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}})+{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}^{(k),j}:={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}^{(k),j}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0),(k),j},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0),(k),j}\rrbracket=\text{HOSVD}_{{\mathbf{r}}}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}^{(k),j})
. Notice that
\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{(k),j}={\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}(\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
.

By
Cai and Zhang 2018
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j\top}\right\|\leq c\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

On the other hand, notice that
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}(\hat{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}}^{(k),j})={\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
. Then by Lemma
24
and Lemma
25
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}\leq cm\kappa\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},

where
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
. Combine the above two inequalities, it has

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0)}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0),(k),j\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq cm\kappa\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

Take maximum over
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
and then we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq cm\kappa\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*}r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

C.2
Entrywise norm

By Lemma
9
, we have the upper bound of the entrywise norm

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq cm^{2}\kappa^{2}\sqrt{r^{*}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{*m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\frac{\Lambda}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},

which finishes the proof.

Lemma 5

(
Yi et al. 2016
)
.

Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
is an
\alpha
-fiber sparse tensor. Then we have

\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}\|\leq\alpha\sqrt{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{S}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Appendix D
Proofs under Missing Values

We shall only prove the following regularity properties with which the convergence dynamics could be obtained easily following the framework of PCA.

Lemma 6

(Two-phase regularity properties with missing data)
.

Suppose
\{\xi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
are i.i.d. following Assumption
3
and independent corruptions
\{s_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
are non-zero with probability
\alpha
. Then there exist
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3},C_{0},C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}
such that if
n\geq C_{0}\bar{d}\log\bar{d}
, then for any fixed
\mu
-incoherent tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
such that
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\gtrsim C_{3}b_{0}\sqrt{\log\bar{d}\cdot d^{*}/n}
for all
k\in[m]
and for any sub-gradient
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\in\partial f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})
, with probability exceeding
1-c_{1}\sum_{l=2,4}\exp(-t_{l}^{2}/(n\|{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}/d^{*}+t_{l}\|{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\|_{\infty}))-c_{2}\sum_{l=1,3}\exp(-t_{l}^{2}/(n\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}/d^{*}+t_{l}\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\|_{\infty}))-c_{3}md^{*-10}
,

1.

we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left(\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-2n\gamma-t_{1};

and for any
k\in[m]
and
j\in[d_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\cdot\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\rangle|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\geq\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d_{k}^{-}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-2\frac{n}{d_{k}}\gamma-t_{2};

2.

we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{4b_{0}}\frac{n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\alpha n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}-t_{3};

and for any
k\in[m]
and
j\in[d_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}mn^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}}\cdot\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+Cn^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}}\cdot\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\rangle|

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}|Y_{i}-\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\rangle|\geq\frac{1}{4b_{0}}\frac{n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\alpha\frac{n}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{\infty}-t_{4};

D.1
Proof of Lemma
6

D.1.1
Phase One Analysis

Analysis of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

Note that with triangle inequality we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}=0\}}

\displaystyle~~~+\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}\neq 0\}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{1}-2(1-\alpha)n\gamma-\alpha n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Denote the event

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}:=\left\{\left|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right]\right|\leq t\right\},

where
c<1/4
is some constant. Specifically, Proposition
1
proves that
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}})\geq 1-2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/d^{*}+t\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\right)
. And event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}
implies that

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-t

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left(\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}\right)-2n\gamma-t,

where Lemma
7
is used.

Analysis of
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

Note that
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
has the expansion,

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{=A_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\underbrace{\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{=A_{2}}.

First analyze
A_{1}
. By sub-gradient definition, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\right\rVert_{1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Notice that
\left\lVert{\mathbf{X}}_{i}\right\rVert_{1}=1
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq n\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}}.

In this way we prove
A_{1}\leq n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}
. Before bounding term
A_{2}
, we introduce the orthogonal matrix
{\mathbf{V}}_{k}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{k}^{-}\times r_{k}}
which denotes
{\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}=\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}
and satisfies
\left\lVert{\mathbf{V}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\mu^{m-1}r_{k}^{-}/d_{k}^{-}}
. Then we have

\displaystyle A_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underbrace{\text{trace}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top})}_{B_{1}}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\underbrace{\sum_{i\neq j}\text{trace}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top})\times\text{sign}(\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle-Y_{i})\times\text{sign}(\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{j},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle-Y_{j})}_{B_{2}}.

We shall only provide the detailed bound of the leading term
B_{2}
. Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i},\xi_{j}^{i},s_{j}^{i}
is an i.i.d. copy of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},\xi_{j},s_{j}
respectively. Denote
C_{ij}^{\prime}:=\text{trace}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i})^{\top})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{j}^{i}-s_{j}^{i})
. Then by decoupling technique
[
De la Pena and Giné 2012
]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|B_{2}\right|\geq t\right)\leq C{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\geq t\right).

First consider
{\mathbb{E}}C_{ij}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}C_{ij}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i})^{\top})\leq\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}.

Also, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(C_{ij}^{\prime})^{2}=\frac{1}{(d^{*})^{2}}\sum_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\in{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}},\,{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}\in{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}}\text{trace}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}){\mathbf{V}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top})^{2}\leq\frac{r_{k}^{2}}{d^{*2}}d_{k},

and
\left|C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\leq\frac{\mu^{m-1}r_{k}^{-}}{d_{k}^{-}}
. Then by Bernstein’s inequality Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}^{\prime}\leq\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}n^{2}}{d^{*}},

holds with probability exceeding
1-\exp(-n^{2}/\bar{d}^{2})
. Thus altogether we have the upper bound
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq Cm\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}n^{2}}{d^{*}}
with probability exceeding
1-m\exp(-n^{2}/\bar{d})
.

Analysis of
f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))

Notice that under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}=0\}}\cdot 1_{\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\in\Omega_{j}^{(k)}\}}

\displaystyle~~~+\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}\neq 0\}}\cdot 1_{\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\in\Omega_{j}^{(k)}\}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{1}-2\frac{n}{d_{k}}\gamma-\frac{\alpha n}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Denote

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)}(t)

\displaystyle:=\left\{\left|f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))-{\mathbb{E}}\left[f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\right]\right|\leq t\right\}.

And with similar proofs in Lemma
1
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)})\geq 1-\exp\left(-c\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+t\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}}\right)
. Then under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)}
, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}^{-1}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~-2\frac{n}{d_{k}}\gamma-\frac{\alpha n}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\infty}-t.

Analysis of
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

Denote
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}:={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
and then we have

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\right)\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle~~~~+\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\neq i,k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\times{\mathbf{V}}_{i}),

where
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}:=\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{i}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}
. Then with similar analyses in
A_{1}
,
A_{2}
and
B_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\cdot n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq mn^{2}C_{1}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\cdot\frac{\mu r}{d_{k}}+C_{2}\frac{n^{2}}{d_{k}d^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq C(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\cdot\frac{\mu r}{d_{k}},

holds with probability exceeding
1-d_{k}\exp(-n^{2}/d_{k}^{2})
. Thus
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\leq C(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\cdot\frac{\mu r}{d_{k}}
holds for all
k=1.\dots,m
with probability exceeding
1-\sum_{k=1}^{m}d_{k}\exp(-n^{2}/d_{k}^{2})
.

D.1.2
Phase Two Analysis

Analysis of
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

Notice that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}=0\}}

\displaystyle~~~+\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}\neq 0\}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\right]-\alpha n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{b_{0}}\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\alpha n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Then under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}
, we have

\displaystyle f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{b_{0}}\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\alpha n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}-t.

Analysis of
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

Note that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{=B_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\underbrace{\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{=B_{2}}.

Also, the sub-gradient has the expression of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i})\cdot{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}
, where
\text{sign}(0)
takes arbitrary values in
[-1,1]
. First consider
B_{1}
term,

\displaystyle B_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}\underbrace{\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{j}-s_{j})}_{C_{ij}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdots\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})^{2}\leq\frac{1}{d^{*}}\cdot d^{*}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{4}\cdots\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{4}=\frac{\mu^{2m}r^{*2}}{d^{*2}}.

\displaystyle\left|\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\right|\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\cdots\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}.

Thus by Bernstein’s inequality Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})-n\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}\right|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{n\mu^{2m}r^{*2}}{d^{*2}}+\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}t}\right).

Take
t=n\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}
and then it leads to

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{k\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\geq 2n\frac{r^{*}}{d^{*}}

holds with probability less than
2\exp(-n/\mu^{2m}r^{*2})
. Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i},\xi_{j}^{i},s_{j}^{i}
is an i.i.d. copy of
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},\xi_{j},s_{j}
respectively. Denote
C_{ij}^{\prime}:=\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i})\cdot\text{sign}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j}^{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{j}^{i}-s_{j}^{i})
. Then by decoupling technique
[
De la Pena and Giné 2012
]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}\right|\geq t\right)\leq C{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\geq t\right).

We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}C_{ij}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=2(1-\alpha)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~\times(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle)-H_{\xi}(0))(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle)-H_{\xi}(0))

\displaystyle+4\alpha(1-\alpha){\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~\times(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-s_{i})-H_{\xi}(0))(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle)-H_{\xi}(0))

\displaystyle+2\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~\times(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-s_{i})-H_{\xi}(0))(H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-s_{j})-H_{\xi}(0))

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\left({\mathbb{E}}H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle)-H_{\xi}(0)\right)^{2}+4\alpha\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[H_{\xi}(\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle)-H_{\xi}(0)\right]

\displaystyle~~~+2\alpha^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{1}^{2}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}+4\alpha\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{1}}{d^{*}b_{1}}+2\alpha^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+4\alpha\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}b_{1}}+2\alpha^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq 3\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}(C_{ij}^{\prime})^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\text{trace}({\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i})\otimes_{j\neq 1}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{1}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{j})^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2m}r^{*2}}{d^{*2}},

\displaystyle\left|C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\leq\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}.

Thus by Bernstein Inequality Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}^{\prime}-{\mathbb{E}}C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{n\mu^{2m}r^{*2}}{d^{*2}}+\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*}}t}\right),

which shows that with probability exceeding
1-\exp(-n)
,

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i\neq j}C_{ij}^{\prime}\right|\leq n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Thus we have
|B_{1}|\leq n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. With similar analyses, we have
|B_{2}|\leq n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}
holds with probability exceeding
1-\sum_{k=1}^{m}\exp(-d_{k})
. Thus in total we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{2}(m+1)n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Analysis of
f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))

Notice that under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-{\mathbb{E}}f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}=0\}}\cdot 1_{\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\in\Omega_{j}^{(k)}\}}

\displaystyle~~~+\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle-\xi_{i}-s_{i}\right|-\left|\xi_{i}+s_{i}\right|\right]\cdot 1_{\{s_{i}\neq 0\}}\cdot 1_{\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{X}}}_{i}\in\Omega_{j}^{(k)}\}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(1-\alpha)n}{d^{*}}\frac{1}{b_{0}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\frac{\alpha n}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Denote

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\left|f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))-{\mathbb{E}}\left[f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\right]\right|\leq t_{j}^{(k)}\right\}.

And with similar proofs in Lemma
7
, we have
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)})\geq 1-\exp\left(-c\frac{n\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d^{*}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}^{2}}\right)
. Then under event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{j}^{(k)}
, we have

\displaystyle f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}))-f({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\geq\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\frac{1}{b_{0}}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\frac{\alpha n}{d_{k}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Analysis of
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

Denote
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}:={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
and then we have

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}+{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\right)\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle~~~~+\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{j\neq i,k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\times{\mathbf{V}}_{i}),

where
{\mathbf{V}}_{i}:=\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{i}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}
. Then with similar analyses in
B_{1}
and
B_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}\cdot C_{1}n^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\left(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq Cmn^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\mu r}{d_{k}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+Cn^{2}\frac{\mu^{m}r^{*}}{d^{*2}b_{1}^{2}}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}^{2},

holds with probability exceeding
1-cd^{*-10}
when
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\geq C_{0}b_{1}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{n}{d^{*}}\log\bar{d}}
.

Proposition 1

(Concentration in the setting of Completion and Independence)
.

Suppose there are
n
pairs of i.i.d. observation,
\{(Y_{i},{\mathbf{X}}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
, satifying
Y_{i}=\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle+\xi_{i}
. Suppose the loss function is given by

f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}):=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|
. Then for any fixed
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
we have with probability exceeding
1-2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d^{*}}+t\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\right)
,

\displaystyle\left|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right]\right|\leq t

Proof.

The proof follows Bernstein’s Inequality Theorem
6
. First note that for any
i=1,\dots,n
,

\displaystyle~~~{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|\right]\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{d^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

At the same time, it has

\displaystyle\left|\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle\right|-\left|Y_{i}-\left\langle{\mathbf{X}}_{i},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rangle\right|\right]\right|\leq 2\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Thus Theorem
6
leads to

\displaystyle\left|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})-{\mathbb{E}}\left[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right]\right|\geq t

holds with probability bounded with
2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{\frac{n\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d^{*}}+t\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\right)
.
∎

Theorem 6

(Bernstein’s Inequality)
.

Let
X_{1},\dots,X_{n}
be independent zero-mean random variables. Suppose that
|X_{i}|\leq M
almost surely, for all
i
. Then for all positive
t>0
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}\big|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{\frac{1}{2}t^{2}}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}X_{i}^{2}+\frac{1}{3}Mt}\right).

Appendix E
Technical Lemma

The following lemma connects
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{1}
,
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\infty}
norm and
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
norm.

Lemma 7
.

For any tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
, its entrywise
\ell_{1}
,
\ell_{\infty}
and Frobenius norm have the following relationship:

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{1}\geq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},\ \ \ \left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{1}\leq\sqrt{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

Notice that we could obtain
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{1}\leq\sqrt{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. Then we only need to discuss the first inequality. Note that Frobenius norm is defined to be
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}:\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle
and suppose it achieves the supremum at
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}
, which implies

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rangle,\quad\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=1,\quad\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})=\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}),\quad\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}/\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{1}=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rangle=\frac{1}{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\infty}\cdot\text{sign}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rangle\geq\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}}\cdot\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rangle=\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}/\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\infty}.

∎

The following lemma analyzes slice sum of the heavy-tailed noise term. Recall that
d^{*}=d_{1}\cdots d_{m}
and
d_{k}^{-}=d^{*}/d_{k}
, for each
k=1,\dots,m
. Also recall that

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{k-1}=1}^{d_{k-1}}\sum_{i_{k+1}=1}^{d_{k+1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}\big|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\big|=:\sum_{i_{k}=j}\big|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\big|.

Lemma 8
.

Suppose random tensor
{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}=\left(\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
contains i.i.d. entries with finite
2+\varepsilon
moment, namely,
{\mathbb{E}}|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|^{2+\varepsilon}<+\infty
. Then for each
k=1,\dots,m
, with probability exceeding
1-c_{1}\frac{d_{k}}{d_{k}^{-}}\cdot\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-\min\{\varepsilon,1\}}-c_{2}\frac{d_{k}}{d_{k}^{-}}\cdot\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-1}
, we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 3\left({\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}\right)^{1/(2+\varepsilon)}\cdot d_{k}^{-},\quad\text{ for all }j=1,\dots,d_{k}.

Proof.

First consider the case when
\varepsilon<1
. For convenience, denote
\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}:=\big|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\big|
. Introduce
\xi
i.i.d. with
\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}
and denote
\gamma:=\left({\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}\right)^{1/(2+\varepsilon)}
. For constant
s>0
, define the truncated variable
\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}:=\big|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\cdot 1_{\{|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|\leq s\}}\big|
. And
\varphi,\bar{\varphi}
are i.i.d. copy of
\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}},\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}
, respectively. Consider the probability of
\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\neq\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\neq\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)={\mathbb{P}}\left(|\xi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}|>s\right)\leq\frac{{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}}{s^{2+\varepsilon}}.

Hence, for the slice, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sum_{i_{k}=j}\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\neq\sum_{i_{k}=j}\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{k}=j}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\neq\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}{d_{k}}\cdot\frac{{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}}{s^{2+\varepsilon}}=d_{k}^{-}\cdot\frac{{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}}{s^{2+\varepsilon}}.

Then consider
\bar{\varphi}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\bigg|\sum_{i_{k}=j}\left[\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}-{\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\right]\bigg|\geq s\right)

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}^{4}\cdot d_{1}\cdots d_{m}/d_{k}+\left({\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}^{2}\right)^{2}\cdot d_{1}^{2}\cdots d_{m}^{2}/d_{k}^{2}}{s^{4}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\frac{{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}\cdot d_{1}\cdots d_{m}/d_{k}}{s^{2+\varepsilon}}+\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}\xi^{2}\right)^{2}\cdot d_{1}^{2}\cdots d_{m}^{2}/d_{k}^{2}}{s^{4}}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}\cdot d_{k}^{-}}{s^{2+\varepsilon}}+\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}\xi^{2}\right)^{2}\cdot(d_{k}^{-})^{2}}{s^{4}},

where the first inequality is from Markov inequality and the second inequality uses
{\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}^{4}\leq s^{2-\varepsilon}{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|^{2+\varepsilon}
. We take
s=d_{k}^{-}\cdot\gamma
and then by the above two equations we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}{\mathbb{P}}\left(\bigg|\sum_{i_{k}=j}\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}-{\mathbb{E}}\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\bigg|\geq 2\gamma\cdot d_{k}^{-}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sum_{i_{k}=j}\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\neq\sum_{i_{k}=j}\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+{\mathbb{P}}\left(\bigg|\sum_{i_{k}=j}\left[\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}-{\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\right]\bigg|\geq\gamma\cdot d_{k}^{-}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-(1+\varepsilon)}+\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-2},

where we use Markov ineuqality,
{\mathbb{E}}\varphi_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}-{\mathbb{E}}\bar{\varphi}_{i_{1}\cdots i_{k-1}ji_{k+1}\cdots i_{m}}\leq\gamma
and
{\mathbb{E}}\xi^{2}\leq\gamma^{2}
.
Hence, take the union for all
j=1,\dots,d_{m}
and then we have with probability exceeding
1-2\frac{d_{k}}{d_{k}^{-}}\cdot\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-\varepsilon}-\frac{d_{k}}{d_{k}^{-}}\cdot\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-1}
, the following holds

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 3\gamma\cdot d_{k}^{-},\quad\text{ for all }j=1,\dots,d_{k}.

Case of
\varepsilon\geq 1
has similar proof where
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\Xi}})\right\rVert_{1}\leq 3\gamma\cdot d_{k}^{-}
holds for all
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
with probability exceeding
1-3\frac{d_{k}}{d_{k}^{-}}\cdot\left(d_{k}^{-}\right)^{-1}
.
∎

Lemma 9
.

Suppose tensors
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu}
have same Tucker rank, with Tucker decomposition
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}
. Introduce matrices
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
, for each
k=1,\dots,m
. Then we have

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r_{1}\cdots r_{m}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m-1}r_{1}\cdots r_{m}/r_{k}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}/d_{k}}}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}.

Proof.

First consider difference between
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
,

\displaystyle{~~~~}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}

\displaystyle=\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{H}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{H}}_{m}\right)\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*})\times_{j>k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{H}}_{j}.

Note that the first term has the expression

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{H}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{H}}_{m}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}=\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top},

which shows

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{H}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{H}}_{m}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m}r_{1}\cdots r_{m}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{m-1}r_{1}\cdots r_{m}/r_{k}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m}/d_{k}}}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

∎

Lemma 10
.

Pseudo-Huber loss function
\rho(x)=\sqrt{x^{2}+\delta^{2}}
maps
{\mathbb{R}}
to
{\mathbb{R}}
. Denote derivative of
\rho(\cdot)
as
\dot{\rho}(\cdot)
and than we have
\dot{\rho}(\cdot)
is Lipschitz continuous with
\delta^{-1}
, namely,

\left|\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})\right|\leq\delta^{-1}\left|x_{1}-x_{2}\right|\quad\text{ for all }x_{1},x_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}},

moreover, we have

\left(\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})\right)^{2}\leq\delta^{-1}(x_{1}-x_{2})(\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})),\quad\text{ for all }x_{1},x_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}.

Proof.

Notice that
\dot{\rho}(x)=\frac{x}{\sqrt{x^{2}+\delta^{2}}}
and second derivative of
\rho(\cdot)
is
\ddot{\rho}(x)=\frac{\delta^{2}}{\left(x^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}
.
\ddot{\rho}
is a bounded function
0\leq\ddot{\rho}(x)\leq\delta^{-1}
. Then for any
x_{1},x_{2}
, we have

\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})=\ddot{\rho}(\theta x_{1}+(1-\theta)x_{2})(x_{1}-x_{2}),

where
\theta\in[0,1]
is some constant. Hence, we have
\left|\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})\right|\leq\delta^{-1}\left|x_{1}-x_{2}\right|
. Then, by
\ddot{\rho}(x)>0
, we have

\left(\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})\right)^{2}\leq\delta^{-1}(x_{1}-x_{2})(\dot{\rho}(x_{1})-\dot{\rho}(x_{2})).

∎

Lemma 11

(Lemma B.8 of
Cai et al. 2022b
)
.

Let
\Omega
be the
\alpha
-fraction set. Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
is
\mu^{*}
-incoherent. Under the assumptions that
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
is
\mu
-incoherent and
\left\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}_{l}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}{16m}
, we have

\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\Omega}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{m}\alpha\max\{\mu^{*},\mu\}^{m}r^{*}\left\|{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

where
C_{m}=4(m+1)
.

E.1
Empirical processes for tensor PCA

Lemma 12
.

Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=\left(\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\right)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
be a random tensor with i.i.d.Rademacher entries, namely,
{\mathbb{P}}(\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}=1)={\mathbb{P}}(\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}=-1)=1/2
. Then there exists some
c>0
such that for all
t>0
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}+C\left(r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}\right)\right).

Specifically, it infers

{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|\leq C\sqrt{r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}.

Proof.

The proof follows
\varepsilon
-net arguments. Suppose it achieves the supremum at
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
,

\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle=\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rangle,

with
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=1
.
Then there exist core tensors
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{1}\times\cdots\times r_{m}}
and orthogonal matrices
{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{1},r_{1}},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{m},r_{m}}
such that

{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)},

Notice that
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=1
. Define
{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}=\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{1}\times\dots\times r_{m}}:\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}=1\}
to be the set of tensors with unit Frobenius norm. Note that
{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}
has one
\varepsilon/(m+1)
-net
{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/(m+1)}^{{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}}
of cardinality
|{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/(m+1)}^{{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}}|\leq(3(m+1)/\varepsilon)^{r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}}
with respect to the Frobenius norm.

Suppose
{\mathcal{N}}_{j}
is
\varepsilon/(m+1)
-nets of orthogonal matrix sets
{\mathbb{O}}_{d_{k},r_{k}}
with respect to
\left\lVert\cdot\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
norm. They have cardinalities

|{\mathcal{N}}_{1}|\leq(3(m+1)/\varepsilon)^{d_{1}r_{1}},\dots,|{\mathcal{N}}_{m}|\leq(3(m+1)/\varepsilon)^{d_{m}r_{m}}.

See
Rauhut et al. 2017
,
Vershynin 2018
for more about
\varepsilon
-nets. Furthermore, the net

{\mathcal{N}}:=\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{V}}_{m}:{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/(m+1)}^{{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}},\,{\mathbf{V}}_{1}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{V}}_{m}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{m}\}

forms a net of
{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}\bigcap\{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}:\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1\}
with cardinality
|{\mathcal{N}}|\leq(3(m+1)/\varepsilon)^{r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}
.

Hence, tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}
has close approximation in the nets. Exist
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{\varepsilon/(m+1)}^{{\mathbb{F}}_{\mathbf{r}}},\,{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\in{\mathcal{N}}_{m}
such that

\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\varepsilon/(m+1),\quad\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\varepsilon/(m+1),\text{ for all }k=1,\dots,m.

Denote the approximation in the nets as
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
. Note that
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
belongs to
{\mathcal{N}}
and it has

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}

\displaystyle=({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{(0)},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}\rrbracket+\sum_{i=1}^{m}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{(0)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{(0)}\rrbracket,

by which we have
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\varepsilon
. Then come back to
\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle
and we have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}\right\rangle|\leq|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle|+|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}_{0}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}}|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|+\varepsilon\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle,

which leads to

\displaystyle\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle\leq\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon}\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}}|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|.

(25)

On the other hand, for any fixed
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}
, we have

\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\dots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}M_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}},

Also, note that

-|M_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}|\leq\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}M_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\leq|M_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}|.

By Hoeffding’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right).

Take the union over
{\mathcal{N}}
and it yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathcal{N}}}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle\geq t\right)\leq 2(3(m+1)/\varepsilon)^{r_{1}r_{2}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\right).

The above equation could be simplified to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle\geq t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}+C\left(r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}\right)\right)

Take
\varepsilon=1/2
and then with Equation (
25
), it verifies

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}},\mu}^{(k)},\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}|\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle|\geq 2t\right)\leq 2\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2}+C\left(r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}\right)\right).

∎

Lemma 13
.

Suppose
f(\cdot)
is given by
f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}):=\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{m}}\rho([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1},\dots,i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}]_{i_{1},\dots,i_{m}})
, where
\rho(\cdot)
is Lipschitz
\tilde{L}
continuous and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{Y}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}+{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}
with independent entries in
{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}
. Then there exist constants
C,C_{1},C_{2}>0
such that,

\displaystyle\bigg|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})]\bigg|\leq\tilde{L}\left(t+C\sqrt{r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}\right)\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

(26)

holds for all
\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
and
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
with probability exceeding
1-\exp\left(-t^{2}/2\right)
.

Proof.

For simplicity, we shall use
T_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}
to represent the
(i_{1},\dots,i_{m})
entry of tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}
. Denote
Z:=\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}}\bigg|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})]\bigg|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}
. First consider
{\mathbb{E}}Z
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}Z

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\sup_{\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}\bigg|f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}[f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}+\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})]\bigg|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}}\bigg|\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\dots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}(\rho(T_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}+\Delta T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}-Y_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}})-\rho(T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}-Y_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}))\bigg|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq 4\tilde{L}{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}}\bigg|\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\dots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\Delta T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}\bigg|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq 4\tilde{L}{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}},\,\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 1}\left\langle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}\right\rangle,

where
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}=(\varepsilon_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}})
is the
d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}
random tensor with i.i.d. Rademacher entries. The second line is from Theorem
7
, the third line is from Theorem
8
. Thus by Lemma
12
we finally get the upper bound of
{\mathbb{E}}Z
.

{\mathbb{E}}Z\leq C\tilde{L}\sqrt{r_{1}\cdots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}.

Note that with Lipschitz continuity of the loss function

\bigg|\rho(T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}+\Delta T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}-Y_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}})-\rho(T_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-Y_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}})\bigg|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1}\leq\tilde{L}|\Delta T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}|\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-1},

and sum of the squared upper bound is
\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}|\Delta T_{i_{1}\dots i_{m}}|^{2}\cdot\left\lVert\Delta{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{-2}=1
.
Then by Theorem
9
, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(Z\geq t\tilde{L}+C\tilde{L}\sqrt{r_{1}r_{2}\dots r_{m}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}r_{j}d_{j}}\right)\leq\exp\left(-t^{2}/2\right).

∎

Theorem 7

(Symmetrization of Expectations,
[
Van Der Vaart et al. 1996
]
)
.

Consider
\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2},\cdots,\mathbf{X}_{n}
independent matrices in
\chi
and let
\mathcal{F}
be a class of real-valued functions on
\chi
. Let
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1},\cdots,\tilde{\varepsilon}_{n}
be a Rademacher sequence independent of
\mathbf{X}_{1},\mathbf{X}_{2},\cdots,\mathbf{X}_{n}
, then

\mathbb{E}\big[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(f(\mathbf{X}_{i})-\mathbb{E}f(\mathbf{X}_{i}))\big|\big]\leq 2\mathbb{E}\big[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\big|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{i}f(\mathbf{X}_{i})\big|\big]

(27)

Theorem 8

(Contraction Theorem,
[
Ludoux and Talagrand 1991
]
)
.

Consider the non-random elements
x_{1},\ldots,x_{n}
of
\chi
. Let
\mathcal{F}
be a class of real-valued functions on
\chi
. Consider the Lipschitz continuous functions
\rho_{i}:\mathbb{R}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
with Lipschitz constant
L
, i.e.

\left|\rho_{i}(\mu)-\rho_{i}(\tilde{\mu})\right|\leq L|\mu-\tilde{\mu}|,\text{ for all }\mu,\tilde{\mu}\in\mathbb{R}

Let
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1},\ldots,\tilde{\varepsilon}_{n}
be a Rademacher sequence
.
Then for any function
f^{*}:\chi\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{i}\left\{\rho_{i}\left(f\left(x_{i}\right)\right)-\rho_{i}\left(f^{*}\left(x_{i}\right)\right)\right\}\right|\right]\leq 2\mathbb{E}\left[L\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mid\sum_{i=1}^{n}\tilde{\varepsilon}_{i}\left(f\left(x_{i}\right)-f^{*}\left(x_{i}\right)\right)\mid\right]

(28)

Theorem 9

(Theorem 12.1 of
Boucheron et al. 2013
)
.

Assume that the sequences of vectors
\left(b_{i,s}\right)_{s\in\mathcal{T}}
and
\left(a_{i,s}\right)_{s\in\mathcal{T}}
,
i=1,\ldots,n
are such that
a_{i,s}\leq X_{i,s}\leq b_{i,s}
holds for all
i=1,\ldots,n
and
s\in\mathcal{T}
with probability 1. Denote

v=\sup_{s\in\mathcal{T}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(b_{i,s}-a_{i,s}\right)^{2}\quad\text{ and }\quad V=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sup_{s\in\mathcal{T}}\left(b_{i,s}-a_{i,s}\right)^{2}.

Then for all
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
,

\log\boldsymbol{E}e^{\lambda(Z-EZ)}\leq\frac{v\lambda^{2}}{2}\quad\text{ and }\quad\log\boldsymbol{E}e^{\lambda(Z-EZ)}\leq\frac{V\lambda^{2}}{8}.

E.2
Expectation of Loss Functions

Lemma 14

(Pseudo-Huber Loss)
.

Suppose the noise assumption
2
holds and
f(\cdot)
is given in Equation (
4
), then for all
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}
we have

{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}})\leq\frac{1}{2\delta}\left|\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|.

Furthermore, if
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{m,\mu,r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)
holds, we have

{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{3b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Proof.

Define
g(t):={\mathbb{E}}\sqrt{(t-\xi)^{2}+\delta^{2}}=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\sqrt{(t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}}\,dH_{\xi}(s)
. Note that

\displaystyle g^{\prime}(t)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{t-s}{\sqrt{(t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}}}\,dH_{\xi}(s),\quad g^{\prime\prime}(t)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}\,dH_{\xi}(s).

According to density
h_{\xi}(\cdot)
condition, we have
g^{\prime}(0)=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{-s}{\sqrt{s^{2}+\delta^{2}}}\,dH_{\xi}(s)=0
. Then for arbitrary
t_{1},t_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}
, we have

\displaystyle g(t_{2})-g(t_{1})

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{t-s}{\sqrt{(t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}}}\,dH_{\xi}(s)\,dt

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{t}{\sqrt{(t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}}}\,dH_{\xi}(s)\,dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\delta}\left|t_{2}^{2}-t_{1}^{2}\right|,

where
\sqrt{(t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}}\geq\delta
is used. Besides, we have the Taylor expansion at
0
using the second order derivative
g^{\prime\prime}(t)
,

\displaystyle g(t_{0})-g(0)=\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{t\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}\,dH_{\xi}(s)\,dt=\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\frac{t\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}\cdot h_{\xi}(s)\,dt\,ds

When
|t_{0}|\leq C_{m,\mu,r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)
, with density lower bound in Assumption
2
, we have

\displaystyle g(t_{0})-g(0)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}\frac{t\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}h_{\xi}(s)\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{t-\delta}^{t+\delta}\frac{t\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}h_{\xi}(s)\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{3\delta\cdot b_{0}}\cdot\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{t-\delta}^{t+\delta}t\,ds\,dt

\displaystyle\geq\frac{t_{0}^{2}}{3b_{0}},

where the third line is because when
|s-t|\leq\delta
,
\frac{\delta^{2}}{\left((t-s)^{2}+\delta^{2}\right)^{3/2}}\geq\frac{1}{3\delta}
. Thus altogether, we get

{\mathbb{E}}\sqrt{(t-\xi)^{2}+\delta^{2}}-{\mathbb{E}}\sqrt{\xi^{2}+\delta^{2}}\geq\frac{t_{0}^{2}}{3b_{0}},\quad{\mathbb{E}}\sqrt{(t_{2}-\xi)^{2}+\delta^{2}}-{\mathbb{E}}\sqrt{(t_{1}-\xi)^{2}+\delta^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2\delta}\left|t_{2}^{2}-t_{1}^{2}\right|,

Then come back to
{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\sqrt{\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)^{2}+\delta^{2}}-\sqrt{\left([{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)^{2}+\delta^{2}}\right].

Thus when
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{m,\mu,r^{*}}(6\gamma+\delta)
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\geq\frac{1}{3b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Similarly, we have

{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathbb{E}}f({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}})\leq\frac{1}{2\delta}\cdot\left|\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{M}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right|.

∎

Lemma 15

(Absolute Loss)
.

Suppose Assumption
2
holds, then for all
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
it has

{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\leq\frac{1}{b_{1}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Furthermore, if
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*},\kappa}\gamma
, it has

{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}\geq\frac{1}{b_{0}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Proof.

Suppose
\xi
satisfies distributions in Assumption
2
. Then we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}|t_{0}-\xi|=2\int_{s>t_{0}}(1-H_{\xi}(s))\,ds+t_{0}-\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}s\,dH_{\xi}(s),

which has more detailed calculationss in
Shen et al. 2023
,
Elsener and van de Geer 2018
. When
t_{0}=0
, it becomes
{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=2\int_{s>0}(1-H_{\xi}(s))\,ds-\int_{-\infty}^{+\infty}s\,dH_{\xi}(s)
. Thus, with
H_{\xi}(0)=1/2
, we have

{\mathbb{E}}|t_{0}-\xi|-{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=2\int_{0}^{t_{0}}H_{\xi}(s)\,ds-t_{0}=2\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{0}^{s}h_{\xi}(x)\,dx\,ds.

Then by Assumption
2
, we have
{\mathbb{E}}|t_{0}-\xi|-{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|\leq\frac{1}{b_{1}}t_{0}^{2}
. Then come back to
{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigg|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\bigg|-\bigg|[{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\bigg|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}\frac{1}{b_{1}}\left([{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=b_{1}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

On the other hand,
h_{\xi}(x)\geq b_{0}^{-1}
when
|x|\leq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*},\kappa}\gamma
. Thus, when
|t_{0}|\leq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*},\kappa}\gamma
, it has

{\mathbb{E}}|t_{0}-\xi|-{\mathbb{E}}|\xi|=2\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\int_{0}^{s}h_{\xi}(x)\,dx\,ds\geq\frac{1}{b_{0}}t_{0}^{2}.

Thus, when
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq C_{m,\mu^{*},r^{*},\kappa}\gamma
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}\right\rVert_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{d_{1}}\cdots\sum_{i_{m}=1}^{d_{m}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\bigg|[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}-[{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\bigg|-\bigg|[{\boldsymbol{\Xi}}]_{i_{1}\cdots i_{m}}\bigg|\right]

\displaystyle\geq b_{0}^{-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

∎

E.3
Perturbation Type Bound

Lemma 16
.

(Matrix Perturbation
Shen et al. 2022
)
Suppose matrix
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times d_{2}}
has rank
r
and has singular value decomposition
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}=\mathbf{U}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}\mathbf{V}^{\top}
where
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}=\operatorname{diag}\{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2},\cdots,\sigma_{r}\}
and
\sigma_{1}\geq\sigma_{2}\geq\cdots\geq\sigma_{r}>0
. Then for any
\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
