satisfying
\|\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}<\sigma_{r}/4
, with
\hat{\mathbf{U}}_{r}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}\times r}
and
\hat{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}\times r}
the left and right singular vectors of
r
largest singular values, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\mathbf{U}}_{r}\hat{\mathbf{U}}_{r}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{\sigma_{r}}\|\hat{\mathbf{M}}-\mathbf{M}\|,\quad\|\hat{\mathbf{V}}_{r}\hat{\mathbf{V}}_{r}^{\top}-\mathbf{V}\mathbf{V}^{\top}\|\leq\frac{4}{\sigma_{r}}\|\hat{\mathbf{M}}-\mathbf{M}\|,

\displaystyle\|\operatorname{SVD}_{r}(\hat{{\mathbf{M}}})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}}\|+20\frac{\|\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|^{2}}{\sigma_{r}},

\displaystyle\|\operatorname{SVD}_{r}(\hat{{\mathbf{M}}})-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}}\|_{\mathrm{F}}+20\frac{\|\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|\|\hat{{\mathbf{M}}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\|_{\mathrm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}}.

Lemma 17
.

Suppose
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times d}
is a symmetric rank
r
matrix, with singular value decomposition
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}={\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}
, where
{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}=\textrm{diag}(\sigma_{1}^{*},\cdots,\sigma_{r}^{*})
,
\sigma_{1}^{*}\geq\cdots\geq\sigma_{r}^{*}>0
. Then for any symmetric matrix satisfying
\left\lVert{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sigma_{r}^{*}/4
with rank
r
singular vector decomposition
\textrm{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{M}})={\mathbf{U}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}
. Denote
{\mathbf{H}}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times r}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{H}}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}.

Proof.

Note that
\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{H}}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}=\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right){\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}
. Denote
\mathbf{Z}=\mathbf{M}-\mathbf{M}^{*}
. Define
\mathbf{U}_{\perp}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times(d-r)}
such that
[\mathbf{U}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{\perp}^{*}]\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
is orthonormal and then define the projector

\mathfrak{P}^{\perp}:=\mathbf{U}_{\perp}^{*}\mathbf{U}_{\perp}^{*\top},\quad\mathfrak{P}^{-1}:=\mathbf{U}\boldsymbol{\Lambda}^{-1}\mathbf{U}^{\top}.

Write
\mathfrak{P}^{-k}=\mathbf{U}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{-k}\mathbf{U}^{*\top}
, for all
k\geq 1
and for convenience when
k=0
, we write
\mathfrak{P}^{0}=\mathfrak{P}^{-1}
. Define the
k
-th order perturbation

\mathcal{S}_{\mathbf{M}^{*},k}(\mathbf{Z}):=\sum_{\mathbf{s}:s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}(-1)^{1+\tau(\mathbf{s})}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{2}}\cdots\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k+1}},

where
s_{1},\cdots,s_{k}
are non-negative integers and
\tau(\mathbf{s})=\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbb{I}(s_{i}>0)
is the number of positive indices in
\mathbf{s}
. Work
Xia 2021
proves

\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}^{*}\mathbf{U}^{*\top}=\sum_{k\geq 1}\mathcal{S}_{\mathbf{M}^{*},k}(\mathbf{Z}).

Then consider
\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right){\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
,

\displaystyle\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right)

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}=\sum_{k\geq 1}\mathcal{S}_{\mathbf{M}^{*},k}(\mathbf{Z}){\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}+\sum_{k\geq 2}\sum_{\mathbf{s}:s_{1}+\cdots+s_{k+1}=k}(-1)^{1+\tau(\mathbf{s})}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{2}}\cdots\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k+1}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}

Note that for
k\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{2}}\cdots\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k+1}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\binom{2k-1}{k}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{k}}{\sigma_{r}^{*k-1}}+\binom{2k-1}{k-1}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{k-1}}{\sigma_{r}^{*k-1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}\leq\sigma_{r}^{*}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left(\frac{4\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}\right)^{k}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left(\frac{4\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}\right)^{k-1}

Hence,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right){\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\sum_{k\geq 2}\left\lVert\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{1}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{2}}\cdots\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k}}\mathbf{Z}\mathfrak{P}^{-s_{k+1}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}.

∎

Lemma 18
.

Suppose
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times d}
is a rank
r
matrix, with singular value decomposition
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}={\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}{\mathbf{V}}^{*\top}
, where
{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}=\textrm{diag}(\sigma_{1}^{*},\cdots,\sigma_{r}^{*})
,
\sigma_{1}^{*}\geq\cdots\geq\sigma_{r}^{*}>0
. Then for any matrix satisfying
\left\lVert{\mathbf{M}}-{\mathbf{M}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sigma_{r}^{*}/4
with rank
r
singular vector decomposition
\textrm{SVD}_{r}({\mathbf{M}})={\mathbf{U}}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}{\mathbf{V}}^{\top}
. Denote
{\mathbf{H}}_{1}={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times r}
and
{\mathbf{H}}_{2}={\mathbf{V}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times r}
. We have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{H}}_{1}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}},

\displaystyle\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{V}}{\mathbf{H}}_{2}-{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{V}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{V}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathbf{Z}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{r}^{*}}

Proof.

Apply Lemma
17
with symmetrization of
{\mathbf{M}}^{*}
and
{\mathbf{M}}
:

\mathbf{Y}^{*}:=\left(\begin{matrix}0&\mathbf{M}^{*}\\
\mathbf{M}^{*\top}&0\end{matrix}\right),\,\mathbf{Y}:=\left(\begin{matrix}0&\mathbf{M}\\
\mathbf{M}^{\top}&0\end{matrix}\right),

and then we could get the desired result.
∎

Lemma 19

(Type-I Tensor Perturbation)
.

Suppose tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
has Tucker rank
{\mathbf{r}}=(r_{1},\dots,r_{m})
. Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*}\times_{2}\dots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}
be its Tucker decomposition with
\underline{\lambda}^{*}:=\min_{k=1,\dots,m}\sigma_{r_{k}}({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))
. Then for any tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
such that
\max_{k=1,\dots,m}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert\leq\underline{\lambda}^{*}/8
with
\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
, then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+32m\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

(29)

Also, for each order
k=1,\dots,m
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},

(30)

and

\begin{split}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}&\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+32m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}\\
&{~~~~~~}+32m\left\lVert({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},\end{split}

(31)

\begin{split}&{~~~}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\\
&\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},\end{split}

(32)

where
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
.

Proof.

Equation (
30
) could be obtained by using Lemma
16
and Euqation (
29
) is from proof of Lemma 13.2 in work
Cai et al. 2022b
. Then we focus on Equation (
32
) and Equation (
31
).

Note that
\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})=\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right){\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})
. Suppose
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
has singular value decomposition
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{*\top}
and its Tucker decomposition matricization is
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\left(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}\right)^{\top}
. It implies

{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})={\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{k}{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{*\top}\left(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}\right).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right){\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+64\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}+16\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}{\mathbf{U}}_{k\perp}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}},

where the last line is from Lemma
18
. Then consider
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
. Work
Cai et al. 2022b
expands
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})
and accordingly we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+32m\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~}+32m\left\lVert({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}

∎

Lemma 20
.

Suppose tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
has Tucker decomposition
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\rrbracket
. Let tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
have
\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
. Denote
\textsf{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}):=\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert
and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}=\arg\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert
. Then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}^{\top},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}^{\top}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\text{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*})+\sqrt{r^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle{~~~~}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}^{\top},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}^{\top}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\times_{1}{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{m}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*\top}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)^{\top}\times_{j>k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}-\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right)\times_{1}{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*\top}\times_{2}\cdots\times_{m}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*\top}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\text{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*})+\sqrt{r^{*}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert.

∎

Lemma 21
.

Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
with Tucker decomposition
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
,
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\rrbracket
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\underline{\lambda}^{*}/8
. Then we have

\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 4m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Furthermore, if
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
are incoherent with parameter
\mu
, namely,
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
,
\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 4(m+1)\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+4m^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle{~~~}+4(m-1)\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle=({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}})({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}-\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textrm{tensor}\left(\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{m}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}-\sum_{k=1}^{m}\textrm{tensor}\left(\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)

Also, we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\textrm{tensor}\left(\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\textrm{tensor}\left(\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)+{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right)

Notice that by Lemma
19
, the first term has the upper bound

\displaystyle\left\lVert\textrm{tensor}\left(\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq 4\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 4m\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\right)\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~}+\sum_{k=1}^{m}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 4m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2},

which uses

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k}({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top})\times_{i<k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\sum_{j=1}^{k-1}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\times_{i<j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*\top}\times_{j}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\right)\times_{i>j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{\top}\times_{k}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(k-1)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

Simialy, we could get upper bound for
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
.
∎

Lemma 22
.

For any two matrices
{\mathbf{U}},{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{d,r}
, denote
{\mathbf{H}}:={\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}
and it has

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{H}}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

By triangle inequality and the inequality
\left\lVert{\mathbf{A}}{\mathbf{B}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{A}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\mathbf{B}}\right\rVert\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{A}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\mathbf{B}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}-{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{H}}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\cdot\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}{\mathbf{U}}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

∎

Lemma 23
.

Suppose
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
with Tucker decomposition
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
,
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\rrbracket
and
\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\underline{\lambda}^{*}/8
. If
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}},{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}
are incoherent with parameter
\mu
, then for tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
and any
k=1,\dots,m
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+(m+1)\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

where
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left(({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+(m+1)\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}=\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\right){\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}=\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\right){\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}.

Denote
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}:={\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}
and we need to bound
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
.
Notice that

\displaystyle\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}

\displaystyle={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textrm{tensor}_{j}\left(\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{j}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+\sum_{j=1}^{m}\textrm{tensor}_{j}\left(\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{j}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\right),

where
\textrm{tensor}_{j}(\cdot):{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{j}\times d_{j}^{-}}\to{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
is inverse of
j
-matricization. First consider
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}
. By Lemma
19
, we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\times_{k=1,\dots,m}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(m-1)\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\cdot\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\cdot\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Then consider
\textrm{tensor}_{j}\left({\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\left((\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}-(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i})^{\top}\right)\right)
, with
j\neq k
. Introduce orthogonal matrices
{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}:=\arg\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert
, for each
k=1,\dots,m
. Then we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\underbrace{{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\left((\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i})^{\top}-(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\right)}_{C}

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\left((\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})^{\top}-(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{l\neq j}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i>l,i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\otimes({\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{l}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{*})^{\top}(\otimes_{i<l,i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})^{\top}}_{C_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\underbrace{{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\sum_{l\neq j}(\otimes_{i>l,i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})\otimes({\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{l}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{*})(\otimes_{i<l,i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})^{\top}}_{C_{2}}

\displaystyle{~~~}+\underbrace{{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})\left({\mathfrak{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})-{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right)(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})^{\top}}_{C_{3}}.

Then, we could bound

\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\textrm{tensor}_{j}(C_{3}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\leq\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

where
j\neq k
. Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\textrm{tensor}_{j}(C_{2}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(m-1)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\textrm{tensor}_{j}(C_{1}))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(m-2)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Altogether, for
j\neq k
we have the upper bound for
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\textrm{tensor}_{j}(C))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
, namely,

\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\textrm{tensor}_{j}(C))\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq 2(m-1)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\|{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left(\textrm{tensor}_{j}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i})^{\top}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle{~~~~~}\left.-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left(\textrm{tensor}_{j}\left({\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\right)\right)\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(2m-1)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Then consider case of
j=k
and similar to
j\neq k
case, it arrives at

\displaystyle{~~~}\left\|\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k})^{\top}\right.

\displaystyle{~~~~~}\left.-\left({\mathbf{I}}_{d_{k}}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\right\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(2m+1)\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

Besides, Lemma
22
implies

\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\leq\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}.

In conclusion, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\right){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+(m+1)\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{H}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}},

which proves the bound for
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right)\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
. Upper bound of
\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}){\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{2,\infty}
would be similar and hence we skip it.
∎

Remark 1
.

Note that if we are only interested in the Frobenius norm of one slice of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}
, namely
\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}
, it has the following bound

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}\left({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right)\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 2m^{2}\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r_{k}}{d_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~}+(m+1)\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}+\underline{\lambda}^{*-1}\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{G}}})\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}

Lemma 24

(Type-II Tensor Perturbation)
.

Suppose tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
has Tucker rank
{\mathbf{r}}=(r_{1},\dots,r_{m})
. Let
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\rrbracket
be its Tucker decomposition. Suppose tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
has
\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
, then
for each order
k=1,\dots,m
and each
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
, we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{2}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

where
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}
,
{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}={\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{*}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k-1}^{*}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*}
,
{\mathbf{W}}_{k}={\mathbf{U}}_{m}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k-1}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{1}
.

Proof.

The proof follows Lemma 4.6.4 of work
Chen et al. 2021a
.

For simplicity, denote
{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}={\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}^{*}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k-1}^{*}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*}
,
{\mathbf{W}}_{k}={\mathbf{U}}_{m}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k+1}\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{k-1}\otimes\dots\otimes{\mathbf{U}}_{1}
and then
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}),{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})
has expression
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}
,
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})={\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}

First, consider the term
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}).

Then consider
{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})
,

\displaystyle{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}.

Combine the above two equations and then we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\top}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}

\displaystyle{~~~~}+{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\left({\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}\right){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}

Note that with
{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}={\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})+{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}
, we could get Equation (
32
). For each
j=1,\dots,d_{k}
, it has

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert\left({\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right)_{j,\cdot}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*})\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\mathbf{W}}_{k}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{\top}-{\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{{\dagger}}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle{~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~}+\left\lVert{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{j}^{(k)}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})\right\rVert_{2}\frac{\left\lVert{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}){\mathbf{W}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

∎

Lemma 25
.

Suppose tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}\in{\mathbb{M}}_{{\mathbf{r}}}
has Tucker decomposition
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}^{*}={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1}^{*},\dots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}^{*}\rrbracket
. Let tensor
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\dots\times d_{m}}
have
\text{HOSVD}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{T}}})={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{U}}_{1},\cdots,{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\rrbracket
. Denote
\text{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}):=\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert
and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}=\arg\min_{{\mathbf{Q}}\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{k},r_{k}}}\left\lVert{\mathbf{U}}_{k}{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}\right\rVert
. Then we have

\displaystyle{~~~~}\left\lVert(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}-(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq i}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*})^{\top}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j\neq k}\text{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*})+8\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}^{\top},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}^{\top}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\right\rVert}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

Proof.

For simplicity, denote
\tilde{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}}:={\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}\rrbracket
. Then we have,

\displaystyle{~~~~}\underbrace{\left((\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{j}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}-(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j})^{\top}\right)}_{C}

\displaystyle=\left((\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{j}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j})^{\top}-(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{l\neq k}(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{i>l,i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*})^{\top}\otimes({\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{l}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{*})^{\top}(\otimes_{i<l,i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})^{\top}}_{C_{1}}

\displaystyle{~~~~}+\underbrace{\sum_{l\neq k}(\otimes_{i>l,i\neq j}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}{\mathbf{Q}}_{i})\otimes({\mathbf{U}}_{l}{\mathbf{Q}}_{l}-{\mathbf{U}}_{l}^{*})(\otimes_{i<l,i\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{i}^{*}){\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j})^{\top}}_{C_{2}}

\displaystyle{~~~~}+\underbrace{(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j})\left({\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}})-{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})^{\dagger}{\mathfrak{M}}_{k}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*})\right)(\otimes_{j\neq k}{\mathbf{U}}_{j}{\mathbf{Q}}_{j})^{\top}}_{C_{3}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\lVert C_{1}\right\rVert\vee\left\lVert C_{2}\right\rVert\leq\sum_{j\neq k}\text{dist}({\mathbf{U}}_{k},{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{*}).

As for term
C_{3}
, note that by Lemma
16
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert C_{3}\right\rVert\leq 8\frac{\left\lVert{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}\cdot\llbracket{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}^{\top},\dots,{\mathbf{Q}}_{m}^{\top}\rrbracket-{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{C}}}^{*}\right\rVert_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\lambda}^{*}}.

∎
